Cobre o que responder e o que deixar (resposta parcial, opinião própria, sem validação automática), os três modos (factual, delicado, grave), como comunicar erro e problema sem se rebaixar (propriedade uma vez em voz de fato, sujeito é o sistema, impacto em vez de intenção, desculpa só com impacto sentido), regras de relação (não expandir escopo, cliente apontando algo não é co… ([sumioshi/wacrew](https://github.com/sumioshi/wacrew/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/comunicacao-cliente) · ★10 · 2 copies)

- **conbio-conservation-relevance-and-implications**: Use when sharpening the conservation significance and actionable implications of a Conservation Biology manuscript. The journal requires results to have direct, transferable implications for conserving biological diversity and to bridge science and practice. Frames the so-what and the recommendations; it does not overstate evidence or fabricate impact. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Conservation-Biology-Skills/skills/conbio-conservation-relevance-and-implications) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **conbio-data-analysis**: Use when executing and reporting the analysis for a Conservation Biology manuscript so it survives expert, double-blind review — appropriate ecological/statistical models, honest uncertainty, robustness, and reproducibility. Covers detection, hierarchical models, spatial structure, and effect sizes that matter for conservation. Guides analysis norms; it does not fabricate results. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Conservation-Biology-Skills/skills/conbio-data-analysis) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **conbio-figures-and-tables**: Use when building tables, figures, and maps for a Conservation Biology manuscript so exhibits are self-contained, accessible, and respect the journal's exhibit budget (about one table or figure per four pages of text). Covers maps, effect plots, and masking sensitive species locations. Designs exhibits; it does not run the analysis. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Conservation-Biology-Skills/skills/conbio-figures-and-tables) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **conbio-literature-positioning**: Use when positioning a Conservation Biology manuscript against the literature so it reads as a novel, transferable contribution to conservation science. Conservation Biology readers span ecology, social science, and practice, so the paper must engage the relevant conservation literatures and state the gap precisely. Stakes the contribution; it does not write the literature review. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Conservation-Biology-Skills/skills/conbio-literature-positioning) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **conbio-reporting-and-data-policy**: Use when preparing the data-availability statement and the data/code archive for a Conservation Biology manuscript. The journal (Wiley/SCB) requires a data-availability statement for research/synthesis articles, encourages data sharing, and requires sensitive species data to be protected. Prepares the package; it does not waive requirements. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Conservation-Biology-Skills/skills/conbio-reporting-and-data-policy) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **conbio-reporting-and-data-policy**: Use when preparing the data-availability statement and the data/code archive for a Conservation Biology manuscript. The journal (Wiley/SCB) expects supporting data and code to be deposited in a public repository such as Dryad with a persistent identifier, and sensitive species data to be protected. Prepares the package; it does not waive requirements. ([franklee16/academic-research-skills](https://github.com/franklee16/academic-research-skills/tree/HEAD/Awesome-Journal-Skills-main/Conservation-Biology-Skills/skills/conbio-reporting-and-data-policy) · ★240)
- **conbio-review-process**: Use to understand how Conservation Biology evaluates a manuscript — double-blind peer review (adopted 2014), editorial screening for scope/novelty/conservation relevance, the editorial-board structure, decision categories, and the Registered Reports route. Sets expectations and shapes the paper to survive review; it does not contact editors. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Conservation-Biology-Skills/skills/conbio-review-process) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **conbio-revision-and-rebuttal**: Use when writing the response to a Conservation Biology revise-and-resubmit. Decisions weigh novelty, transferability, methodological soundness, and direct conservation relevance, so the response must convert each reviewer while keeping the handling editor confident. Structures the response letter; it does not fabricate new results. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Conservation-Biology-Skills/skills/conbio-revision-and-rebuttal) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **conbio-study-design**: Use when defending the study design of a Conservation Biology manuscript — ecological sampling and inference (detection, spatial structure, pseudoreplication), comparative/observational and quasi-experimental designs (BACI), modeling and synthesis design, and human-dimensions methods. The journal judges each appropriate method on its own terms. Strengthens the design; it does not write code. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Conservation-Biology-Skills/skills/conbio-study-design) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **conbio-submission**: Use when running the final pre-submission preflight for Conservation Biology via Wiley's ScholarOne Manuscript Central — article-type selection, double-blind preparation, word/abstract caps, Style Guide formatting, data-availability statement, and declarations. Final checks; it does not draft content. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Conservation-Biology-Skills/skills/conbio-submission) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **conbio-topic-selection**: Use when deciding whether a project fits Conservation Biology and which article type to target. Helps frame the question; it does not collect data. - **conbio-workflow**: Use as the entry point for any Conservation Biology manuscript. - **concept-check**: 创意与选题检查工具，检测题材匹配度、辨识度、混搭合理性等问题。当用户说"选题检查"、"创意检查"、"题材检查"、"检查选题"时自动激活。 ([hestudy/snowflake-fiction](https://github.com/hestudy/snowflake-fiction/tree/HEAD/skills/concept-check) · ★37)
- **concept-check**: 概念核验。两种深度：A 贴身词语核验（写文案时一个词该不该留，三问自检 + 行业黑话豁免）；B 深度概念拆解（把一个模糊概念拆到说不清就是没想清——对齐是否各说各话、往回倒查原意、假设检查戳穿伪概念、检验是描述行动还是替代了行动、去词测试、判断是信息缺口还是执行缺口）。别只停在"是不是 AI 空词"就收工，那只是 A 模式；用户要"深度拆解"必须走完 B 模式。 ([MarchTalk/ai-content-skills](https://github.com/MarchTalk/ai-content-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/concept-check) · ★4 · 3 copies)
- **concept-check**: Socratic quiz to test understanding of concepts from the learning journals.
